data-cds-segment="" class="contents"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-fill-secondary group-hover/btn:bg-fill-secondary-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-secondary-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" class="text-muted" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></div></div></div><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></div></div><p>The tool search tool lets Claude work with hundreds or thousands of tools by discovering and loading them on demand. Instead of loading all tool definitions into the context window up front, Claude searches your tool catalog (including tool names, descriptions, argument names, and argument descriptions) and loads only the tools it needs.</p>
<p>Loading every tool definition up front causes two problems as a tool library grows:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Context bloat:</strong> A typical multiserver setup (GitHub, Slack, Sentry, Grafana, and Splunk) can consume ~55k tokens in definitions before Claude does any work. Tool search typically reduces this by over 85 percent, loading only the 3–5 tools Claude needs for a given request.</li>
<li><strong>Tool selection accuracy:</strong> Claude&#x27;s ability to pick the right tool degrades once you exceed 30–50 available tools. Because tool search loads only a focused set of relevant tools on demand, selection accuracy stays high even across thousands of tools.</li>
</ul>
<p>For the models that support tool search, see <a href="#model-compatibility">Model compatibility</a>.</p>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>For background on the scaling challenges that tool search solves, see <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/advanced-tool-use" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Advanced tool use</a>. Tool search&#x27;s on-demand loading is also an instance of the broader just-in-time retrieval principle described in <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/effective-context-engineering-for-ai-agents" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Effective context engineering</a>.</p></div></aside>
<p>Tool search runs as a server-side tool, but you can also implement your own client-side tool search. See <a href="#custom-tool-search-implementation">Custom tool search implementation</a> for details.</p>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>Share feedback on this feature through the <a href="https://forms.gle/MhcGFFwLxuwnWTkYA" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">feedback form</a>.</p></div></aside>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>To learn how zero data retention (ZDR) applies to this feature, see <a href="/docs/en/manage-claude/api-and-data-retention">API and data retention</a>.</p></div></aside>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-warning text-warning shadow-ring-warning [--cds-prose-code-color:currentColor] [--cds-prose-marker-color:currentColor] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>On Amazon Bedrock, server-side tool search is available only through the
<a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/bedrock-runtime_example_bedrock-runtime_InvokeModel_AnthropicClaude_section.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">InvokeModel
API</a>,
not the Converse API.</p></div></aside>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>On <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-platform-on-aws">Claude Platform on AWS</a>, server-side tool search works identically to the Claude API. Claude Platform on AWS uses the Anthropic Messages API directly, so there is no InvokeModel or Converse distinction.</p></div></aside>

<!-- -->
<p>Claude Opus 4.1 and earlier models don&#x27;t support the tool search tool.</p>
<h2 id="how-tool-search-works" class="group">How tool search works<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_23r6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>There are two tool search variants:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Regex</strong> (<code>tool_search_tool_regex_20251119</code>): Claude constructs regex patterns to search for tools.</li>
<li><strong>BM25</strong> (<code>tool_search_tool_bm25_20251119</code>): Claude uses natural language queries to search for tools.</li>
</ul>
<p>When you enable the tool search tool:</p>
<ol>
<li>You include a tool search tool (for example, <code>tool_search_tool_regex_20251119</code> or <code>tool_search_tool_bm25_20251119</code>) in your <code>tools</code> list.</li>
<li>You provide every tool definition in the <code>tools</code> array and set <code>defer_loading: true</code> on the tools that shouldn&#x27;t load up front. At least one tool, normally the tool search tool itself, must stay non-deferred.</li>
<li>Initially, Claude&#x27;s context contains only the tool search tool and any non-deferred tools.</li>
<li>When Claude needs additional tools, it searches using a tool search tool.</li>
<li>The API runs the search and returns the matching tools as <code>tool_reference</code> blocks (up to 5 by default; Claude can set a <code>limit</code> in its search input).</li>
<li>The API automatically expands these references into full tool definitions.</li>
<li>Claude selects from the discovered tools and calls them.</li>
</ol>

<p>Claude searches the catalog, discovers <code>get_weather</code>, and calls it. The response ends with <code>stop_reason: &quot;tool_use&quot;</code>. Execute the discovered tool and return a <code>tool_result</code> as in <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/handle-tool-calls">Handle tool calls</a>. <a href="#response-format">Response format</a> shows the blocks you get back and what to send next.</p>

<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-warning text-warning shadow-ring-warning [--cds-prose-code-color:currentColor] [--cds-prose-marker-color:currentColor] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p><strong>Regex variant query format: Python regex, not natural language</strong></p><p>With <code>tool_search_tool_regex_20251119</code>, Claude writes Python <code>re.search()</code> patterns, not natural language queries. Matching is case-insensitive. Common patterns include the following:</p><ul>
<li><code>&quot;weather&quot;</code>: matches tool names and descriptions containing &quot;weather&quot;</li>
<li><code>&quot;get_.*_data&quot;</code>: matches tools such as <code>get_user_data</code> and <code>get_weather_data</code></li>
<li><code>&quot;database.*query|query.*database&quot;</code>: matches either word order</li>
</ul><p>Maximum pattern length: 200 characters</p></div></aside>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p><strong>BM25 variant query format: natural language</strong></p><p>With <code>tool_search_tool_bm25_20251119</code>, Claude searches with natural language queries. Maximum query length: 500 characters.</p></div></aside>
<h3 id="deferred-tool-loading" class="group">Deferred tool loading<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_27n6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p>Mark tools for on-demand loading by adding <code>defer_loading: true</code>:</p>

aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></div></div><div data-copy-scope="" class="code-block-scroll relative py-4 pl-4 text-code overflow-x-auto" tabindex="0"><div class="inline-block min-w-full pr-4" data-code-source="" translate="no"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" <span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "input_schema"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    "required"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"location"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "defer_loading"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p><code>defer_loading</code> controls what enters the context window, not what you send in the request:</p>
<ul>
<li>You still send every tool&#x27;s full definition in the <code>tools</code> array on every request, including the deferred ones. The API needs them server-side to run the search and expand <code>tool_reference</code> blocks.</li>
<li>Tools without <code>defer_loading</code> load into context immediately.</li>
<li>Tools with <code>defer_loading: true</code> load only when Claude discovers them through search.</li>
<li>Never set <code>defer_loading: true</code> on the tool search tool itself.</li>
<li>Keep your 3–5 most frequently used tools non-deferred so Claude can call them without searching first.</li>
</ul>
<p>The computer use and browser use toolsets (<code>computer_toolset_20260801</code> and <code>browser_toolset_20260801</code>) take <code>defer_loading</code> per member tool inside the entry&#x27;s <code>configs</code> object, not on the entry itself; a request that sets it at the entry level is rejected. Because a toolset defers and expands as a unit, <code>defer_loading</code> must resolve to the same value on every enabled member, and when Claude discovers the toolset through search, every enabled member loads at once. See <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/tool-reference#client-toolsets">Client toolsets</a> for the <code>configs</code> format.</p>
<p>Both tool search variants (<code>regex</code> and <code>bm25</code>) search tool names, descriptions, argument names, and argument descriptions.</p>
<p>Internally, the API excludes deferred tools from the system-prompt prefix. When Claude discovers a deferred tool through tool search, the API appends a <code>tool_reference</code> block inline in the conversation, then expands it into the full tool definition before passing it to Claude. The prefix is untouched, so prompt caching is preserved. The grammar for <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/strict-tool-use">strict mode</a> (the rules that constrain tool-call output to match your schemas) builds from the full toolset, so <code>defer_loading</code> and strict mode compose without grammar recompilation.</p>
<h2 id="response-format" class="group">Response format<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_29n6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_search_tool_result"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "tool_use_id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"srvtoolu_01ABC123"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "content"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_search_tool_search_result"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        "tool_references"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: [{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_reference"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"tool_name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"get_weather"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6"> }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"text"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "text"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"I found a weather tool. Let me get the weather for San Francisco."</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_use"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"toolu_01XYZ789"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"get_weather"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      "input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"location"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"San Francisco"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"unit"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"fahrenheit"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">  ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "stop_reason"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_use"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h3 id="understanding-the-response" class="group">Understanding the response<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2af6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>server_tool_use</code>:</strong> Claude&#x27;s call to the tool search tool. The search runs on Anthropic&#x27;s servers. Never return a <code>tool_result</code> for its <code>srvtoolu_...</code> ID. The <code>input</code> holds the search (<code>pattern</code> for the regex variant, <code>query</code> for BM25) and may include an optional <code>limit</code>, an integer from 1 to 10,000 that caps how many matching tools the search returns (default: 5).</li>
<li><strong><code>tool_search_tool_result</code>:</strong> the search results, in a nested <code>tool_search_tool_search_result</code> object. Keep it in the message history as is.</li>
<li><strong><code>tool_references</code>:</strong> an array of <code>tool_reference</code> objects pointing to discovered tools. The API expands these for Claude. You never expand them yourself.</li>
<li><strong><code>tool_use</code>:</strong> Claude&#x27;s call to a discovered tool. Execute it and return a <code>tool_result</code> exactly as in standard tool use.</li>
</ul>
<p>The API automatically expands <code>tool_reference</code> blocks into full tool definitions before showing them to Claude. You don&#x27;t need to handle this expansion yourself, as long as you provide all matching tool definitions in the <code>tools</code> parameter.</p>
<h3 id="continuing-the-conversation" class="group">Continuing the conversation<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2b76aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p>On the next request, pass the assistant&#x27;s content back unchanged, including the <code>server_tool_use</code> and <code>tool_search_tool_result</code> blocks. Add your <code>tool_result</code> for the discovered tool in a user message, and send the same <code>tools</code> array: the search tool plus every deferred definition. Don&#x27;t return a <code>tool_result</code> for the <code>srvtoolu_...</code> ID: the API rejects the request. The API expands <code>tool_reference</code> blocks throughout the conversation history, so Claude can reuse discovered tools in later turns without re-searching. A search that matches nothing returns a <code>tool_search_tool_search_result</code> with an empty <code>tool_references</code> array, not an error.</p>
<h2 id="mcp-integration" class="group">MCP integration<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2bn6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<!-- -->
<p>If your tools come from MCP servers through the <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/mcp-connector">MCP connector</a>, you don&#x27;t set <code>defer_loading</code> on individual tool definitions. Instead, set it once on the <code>mcp_toolset</code> entry&#x27;s <code>default_config</code> for the whole server, or per tool in its <code>configs</code>. See <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/mcp-connector#mcp-toolset-configuration">MCP toolset configuration</a>.</p>
<h2 id="custom-tool-search-implementation" class="group">Custom tool search implementation<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2cb6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>You can implement your own tool search logic (for example, using embeddings or semantic search) by returning <code>tool_reference</code> blocks from a custom tool. When Claude calls your custom search tool, return a standard <code>tool_result</code> with <code>tool_reference</code> blocks in the content array:</p>
<div data-not-prose="true" class="overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-surface-2 group relative"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b px-4 py-1"><div class="flex min-w-0 duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></div></div><div data-copy-scope="" class="code-block-scroll relative py-4 pl-4 text-code overflow-x-auto" tabindex="0"><div class="inline-block min-w-full pr-4" data-code-source="" translate="no"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" <span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_result"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "tool_use_id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"toolu_your_tool_id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "content"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: [{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_reference"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"tool_name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"discovered_tool_name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6"> }]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>Every tool referenced must have a corresponding tool definition in the top-level <code>tools</code> parameter, normally with <code>defer_loading: true</code>. This lets you use search methods the built-in variants don&#x27;t provide, such as embedding-based retrieval, and the API expands the returned <code>tool_reference</code> blocks the same way.</p>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>The <code>tool_search_tool_result</code> format shown in the <a href="#response-format">Response format</a> section is the server-side format used internally by Anthropic&#x27;s built-in tool search. For custom client-side implementations, always use the standard <code>tool_result</code> format with <code>tool_reference</code> content blocks as shown in the preceding example.</p></div></aside>
<p>For a complete example using embeddings, see the <a href="https://platform.claude.com/cookbook/tool-use-tool-search-with-embeddings" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tool search with embeddings</a> recipe.</p>
<h2 id="error-handling" class="group">Error handling<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2dr6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body font-normal bg-surface-1 text-primary shadow-card-ring [--cds-prose-code-color:var(--cds-text-danger)] [--cds-prose-marker-color:var(--cds-text-secondary)] prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center text-secondary"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p><a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/define-tools#providing-tool-use-examples">Tool use examples</a>
work with tool search: when Claude discovers a deferred tool, the API expands
its <code>input_examples</code> along with its definition.</p></div></aside>

<h3 id="tool-result-errors-200-status" class="group">Tool result errors (200 status)<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2fr6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p>When a tool search operation fails during execution, the API returns a 200 response with the error in the body:</p>
<div data-not-prose="true" class="overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-surface-2 group relative"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b px-4 py-1"><div class="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-caption text-muted">JSON</div><div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="sm" [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 text-muted" aria-label="Copy code" id="_R_3pgb6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></div></div><div data-copy-scope="" class="code-block-scroll relative py-4 pl-4 text-code overflow-x-auto" tabindex="0"><div class="inline-block min-w-full pr-4" data-code-source="" translate="no"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="--shiki-light:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6;--shiki-light-bg:transparent;--shiki-dark-bg:transparent"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_search_tool_result"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "tool_use_id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"srvtoolu_01ABC123"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  "content"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    "type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_search_tool_result_error"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    "error_code"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"invalid_tool_input"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    "error_message"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"Invalid regular expression pattern: missing ) at position 1"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>The <code>error_code</code> field has four possible values:</p>
<ul>
<li><code>invalid_tool_input</code>: the search input was invalid, for example a malformed regex pattern or a pattern over the 200-character limit</li>
<li><code>unavailable</code>: the search couldn&#x27;t run, for example because it timed out or the service was unavailable</li>
<li><code>too_many_requests</code>: rate limit exceeded for tool search operations</li>
<li><code>execution_time_exceeded</code>: the search exceeded its execution time limit</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="common-mistakes" class="group">Common mistakes<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2h36aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<div data-orientation="vertical" data-cds="Accordion" class="font-sans divide-y divide prose-block" style="--accordion-px:var(--cds-pad-md);--accordion-radius:var(--cds-radius)"><div select-text"><span class="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words">Claude doesn&#x27;t find expected tools</span><span class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1.25rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:433.3" class="text-muted transition-transform group-data-[panel-open]/acc:rotate-90" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></h3><div data-orientation="vertical" data-hidden="" data-index="-1" data-closed="" data-starting-style="" hidden="" id="base-ui-_R_hr6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plbH1_" aria-labelledby="base-ui-_R_hr6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plbH2_" role="region" style="--accordion-panel-height:auto;--accordion-panel-width:auto" class="overflow-hidden font-normal text-secondary h-[var(--accordion-panel-height)] transition-[height] duration-base ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none data-[starting-style]:h-0 data-[ending-style]:h-0 text-prose"><div class="py-md"><p><strong>Cause:</strong> The regex pattern doesn&#x27;t match the tool&#x27;s name, description, argument names, or argument descriptions.</p><p><strong>Debugging steps:</strong></p><ol>
<li>Check tool name, description, argument names, and argument descriptions. Claude searches all of these fields.</li>
<li>Test your pattern: <code>import re; re.search(r&quot;your_pattern&quot;, &quot;tool_name&quot;, re.IGNORECASE)</code>.</li>
<li>Matching is case-insensitive, so casing differences aren&#x27;t the problem.</li>
<li>Claude uses broad patterns such as <code>&quot;.*weather.*&quot;</code>, not exact matches.</li>
</ol><p><strong>Tip:</strong> Add common keywords to tool descriptions to improve discoverability.</p></div></div></div></div>
<h2 id="prompt-caching" class="group">Prompt caching<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2i36aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<!-- -->
<p>To learn how <code>defer_loading</code> preserves prompt caching, see <a href="/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/tool-use-with-prompt-caching">Tool use with prompt caching</a>.</p>
<p>A tool with <code>defer_loading: true</code> can&#x27;t also carry <code>cache_control</code>: the API returns a 400. Put the cache breakpoint on a non-deferred tool.</p>
<h2 id="streaming" class="group">Streaming<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2iv6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>With streaming enabled, you&#x27;ll receive tool search events as part of the stream:</p>
<div data-not-prose="true" class="overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-surface-2 group relative"><div data-copy-scope="" class="code-block-scroll relative py-4 pl-4 text-code overflow-x-auto" tabindex="0"><div class="inline-block min-w-full pr-4" data-code-source="" translate="no"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="--shiki-light:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6;--shiki-light-bg:transparent;--shiki-dark-bg:transparent"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">event: content_block_start</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">data: {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"content_block_start"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"index"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"content_block"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"server_tool_use"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"id"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"srvtoolu_xyz789"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"name"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"tool_search_tool_regex"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// Search pattern streamed</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">event: content_block_delta</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">data: {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"content_block_delta"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"index"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"delta"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"type"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"input_json_delta"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">"partial_json"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">"{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D">\"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">pattern</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D">\"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D">\"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">weather</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D">\"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#f3f7f6">}}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// Pause while search executes</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// Search results streamed</span></span>

<h2 id="batch-requests" class="group">Batch requests<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2jn6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>You can include the tool search tool in the <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/batch-processing">Messages Batches API</a>.</p>
<h2 id="limits-and-best-practices" class="group">Limits and best practices<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2k76aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<h3 id="limits" class="group">Limits<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2kf6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Maximum deferred tools:</strong> 10,000 tools with <code>defer_loading: true</code> per request</li>
<li><strong>Search results:</strong> each search returns up to 5 matching tools by default; Claude can set <code>limit</code> in its search input to any integer from 1 to 10,000</li>
<li><strong>Pattern and query length:</strong> maximum 200 characters for regex patterns and 500 characters for BM25 queries</li>
<li><strong>Model support:</strong> see <a href="#model-compatibility">Model compatibility</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 id="when-to-use-tool-search" class="group">When to use tool search<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2kv6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<p>Use tool search when any of the following apply:</p>
<ul>
<li>You have 10 or more tools available.</li>
<li>Your tool definitions consume more than 10k tokens.</li>
<li>Tool selection accuracy drops as your toolset grows.</li>
<li>You aggregate multiple MCP servers (200+ tools).</li>
<li>Your tool library grows over time.</li>
</ul>
<p>Standard tool calling, without tool search, is a better fit when you have fewer than 10 tools, every tool is used in every request, or your tool definitions are small (less than 100 tokens total).</p>
<h3 id="optimization-tips" class="group">Optimization tips<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2lv6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h3>
<ul>
<li>Keep your 3–5 most frequently used tools non-deferred.</li>
<li>Write clear, descriptive tool names and descriptions.</li>
<li>Use consistent namespacing in tool names: prefix by service or resource (for example, <code>github_</code>, <code>slack_</code>) so one search matches the whole group.</li>
<li>Use keywords in descriptions that match how users describe tasks.</li>
<li>Add a system prompt section describing available tool categories: &quot;You can search for tools to interact with Slack, GitHub, and Jira.&quot;</li>
<li>Monitor which tools Claude discovers to refine your descriptions.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="usage" class="group">Usage<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2mf6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Tool search isn&#x27;t metered as a separate server tool. The response&#x27;s <code>usage.server_tool_use</code> object has no tool search field, and the tool definitions that search loads into context count as input tokens like any other tool definition.</p>
<h2 id="next-steps" class="group">Next steps<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_2mv6aav5ul5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-size:calc(1rem * var(--cds-rem-scale, 1));font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
